Oct 25 13:40:54 evo-x2 gatus[3422689]: 2026/10/25 13:40:54 [watchdog.handleAlertsToTrigger] Sending initial custom alert because alert for endpoint=Memory Guard Trip Churn with description='Memory guard TRIP CHURN (>=6 trips in the last hour — the 2026-09-14 Zone 6 storm class; per-trip Discord pings are suppressed while this is active, this is the ONLY storm alert): a repeating trip→restore→re-trip loop is burning the restore budget and the machine is in a sustained storm. This is one message for the whole storm, NOT one per trip. Check, in order: (1) memory_emergency_guard_zone_trips_last_hour — which zone dominates; (2) the guard's per-trip io.stat attribution lines: journalctl -u memory-emergency-guard --since -2h | grep 'top io'; (3) whether the sev1 desktop notify-tier churn context actually fired: journalctl -u sev1-bridge --since -2h | grep -i churn (it gates at >=2 trips/hour — if absent, the bridge is dead and Discord is the only channel); (4) restore state: memory_emergency_guard_restore_capped / trips_last_hour. Do NOT chase individual trips — shed the driver load the io.stat lines name, or wait for the drain (churn_mode returns to 0 and this alert resolves).' has been TRIGGERED
Oct 25 13:40:54 evo-x2 gatus[3422689]: 2026/10/25 13:40:54 [watchdog.handleAlertsToTrigger] Sending initial custom alert because alert for endpoint=Memory Emergency Guard with description='Memory emergency guard TRIPPED (or the guard died): the machine entered a pre-freeze zone (low MemAvailable, near-full zram, PSI refault thrash, episodic memory stall, or sustained I/O stall with disk-busy corroboration) and FastFlowLM + its activation socket + the resumable I/O churn units (btrbk/balance/scrub) were force-stopped. The socket auto-restores once memory recovers; until then LLM clients get connection-refused by design. If memory_emergency_guard_churn_mode is 1 this alert is SUPPRESSED (see 'Memory Guard Trip Churn'). Oct 25 17:01:16 evo-x2 gatus[289493]: 2026/10/25 17:01:16 [watchdog.handleAlertsToTrigger] Sending initial discord alert because alert for endpoint=Memory Emergency Guard with description='Memory emergency guard TRIPPED (or the guard died): the machine entered a pre-freeze zone (low MemAvailable, near-full zram, PSI refault thrash, episodic memory stall, or sustained I/O stall with disk-busy corroboration) and FastFlowLM + its activation socket + the resumable I/O churn units (btrbk/balance/scrub) were force-stopped. ## Remote log tail — ClickHouse death window (signoz_logs.distributed_logs_v2)

Death window per M22: [journal cut -> next boot], epoch-ns UTC. At a watchdog death the local journal loses its final ~30-90s to flush lag; when the OTel->ClickHouse pipeline is alive the remote tail is FRESHER than the journal cut (#36: newest row 5s before the cut), and when the collector died pre-death the pipeline is silently DARK (#37: 4h19m outage).
Window: 1792963333000000000 -> 1792963421000000000 epoch-ns UTC (2026-10-25 21:22:13 -> 2026-10-25 21:23:41 UTC)
PIPELINE DARK FOR THIS WINDOW — 0 rows in the window.
Per M22: ClickHouse absence is NOT evidence of absence — the collector has a fatal-at-startup failure mode that silences the whole log pipeline (proven #37). Cross-check signoz-collector liveness across this boot BEFORE trusting the gap.
```
```
